原文：A note from the Knitting Circle; Collective Mayor of Paint Town;

With seas lapping at its coast, the Town of paint is an idyllic spot, beautiful at most times of the year. With kind people and quaint shops, it’s a wonderful destination for short or prolonged visits.

Make sure to say hello to the birds, they prefer to be addressed by ernie, but will also accept bird. We do advise you to keep your distance from the bee’s, they scare easy and it’s rude to scare strangers.

But we digress, we have a job for you. 

We would like you to re-colour our town, it was all washed away last winter storm and is in dire need of a new lick of paint. Dont worry too much about doing it “right”, we don't mind just have fun and give it some life.

We’ll like whatever you do.

Signed;
The Knitting Circle
